```js
function onNet(m) {
  switch (m.t) {
    case 'welcome':
      NET.seat = m.seat === 1 ? 1 : 0; NET.peer = !!m.peer; NET.link = 'online'; updateNetBadge();
      lobbyStatus(NET.peer ? 'Connected. Starting…' : waitText());
      if (NET.peer) sendHello();
      if (state !== 'lobby') updateHUD();
      break;
    case 'full':
      clearRejoin();
      leaveOnline(); toMenu(); menuNote('That room already has two players in it.'); break;
    case 'peer':
      NET.peer = !!m.on; updateNetBadge();
      if (m.on) sendHello();
      else if (NET.started) toast(`${NET.peerName} disconnected. Waiting for them to come back`, 'foul');
      else lobbyStatus(waitText());
      break;
    case 'hello':
      NET.peerName = cleanName(m.name); NET.peer = true; updateNetBadge(); takeLooks(m);
      // versions before this check sent no version, so a missing one means an older copy
      NET.peerVer = typeof m.v === 'string' ? m.v.slice(0, 16) : '';
      NET.verWarn = '';
      if (NET.peerVer !== BUILD) {
        const why = NET.peerVer ? `You and ${NET.peerName} have different versions of Retro Rack.` : `${NET.peerName} has an older version of Retro Rack.`;
        if (!NET.started && !m.started) {   // nothing to lose yet, so don't start a game that might not match
          lobbyStatus(`${why} ${NET.peerVer ? 'Both of you reload' : 'Ask them to reload'} it to get the latest (Ctrl+F5 on a computer, or close and reopen it on a phone), then come back to room ${NET.code}.`);
          break;
        }
        // mid-game: carry on (the after-shot cross-check keeps the tables matched) but say so
        NET.verWarn = `${why} If the tables stop matching, reload it (Ctrl+F5 on a computer, or close and reopen it on a phone) and come back to room ${NET.code}.`;
        if (NET.started) toast(NET.verWarn, 'foul');   // a player rejoining sees it once the table is back (adoptState)
      }
      if (NET.started) netSendState();
      else if (NET.seat === 0 && !m.started) hostStart();
      else lobbyStatus(`Connected to ${NET.peerName}. Starting…`);
      if (state !== 'lobby') updateHUD();
      break;
    case 'setup': if (NET.seat === 1 && (NET.started || NET.peerVer === BUILD)) startOnlineGame(m); break;
    case 'state': adoptState(m); break;
    case 'look': takeLooks(m); break;
    case 'aim': if (state === 'remote') NET.aimT = m; break;
    case 'shot': if (typeof m.n === 'number') { NET.queue.push(m); NET.queue.sort((a, b) => a.n - b.n); netProcessQueue(); } break;
    case 'sync': if (typeof m.n === 'number' && Array.isArray(m.balls) && m.game) { NET.pendingSync[m.n] = m; netCheckSync(); } break;
    case 'again': NET.again[1 - NET.seat] = true; tryRematch(); break;
    case 'bye': NET.peer = false; updateNetBadge(); clearRejoin(); toast(`${NET.peerName} left the game`, 'foul'); break;
    case 'chat': if (Number.isInteger(m.i) && CHAT[m.i]) { toast(`${NET.peerName}: ${CHAT[m.i]}`, 'chat'); sfx('ui'); } break;
    case 'concede':
      if (state === 'moving' || state === 'stroke') NET.pendingConcede = m.from === 1 ? 1 : 0;
      else concedeFrame(true, m.from === 1 ? 1 : 0);
      break;
    case 'rerackOffer': if (!game.over) { $('#offerTxt').textContent = `${NET.peerName} offers a re-rack. This frame won't count.`; $('#offer').hidden = false; } break;
    case 'rerackYes': toast(`${NET.peerName} accepted the re-rack`, 'good'); if (NET.seat === 0) hostStart(); break;
    case 'rerackNo': toast(`${NET.peerName} declined the re-rack`, 'info'); break;
  }
}
function menuNote(t) { $('#netNote').textContent = t; }
function lobbyStatus(t) { $('#lobbyStatus').textContent = t; }
function inviteLink() { return location.href.split('#')[0] + '#room=' + NET.code; }
function startOnline(code, listed = false, rejoin = null) {
  if (!relayBase()) { menuNote('Online play needs your relay address first. Paste it into config.js, next to this file (the README explains how).'); return; }
  if (!code || code.length < 4) { menuNote('Room codes are 5 letters and numbers.'); return; }
  NET.on = true; NET.code = code; NET.cid = getCid(); NET.myName = cleanName($('#netName').value, 'Player'); NET.peerName = 'Friend';
  try { localStorage.setItem('retroRack.name', NET.myName); } catch (e) {}
  holdSeat();
  Object.assign(NET, { started: false, n: 0, games: 0, queue: [], pendingSync: {}, again: [false, false], retry: 0, peer: false, peerVer: '', verWarn: '', aimT: null, stateAfter: false, list: listed, rejoin, cloth: null, peerCue: 'house' });
  matchWins = [0, 0]; M.opp = 'online'; saveM(); menuNote(''); NAV.lastOnline = true;
  try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#room=' + code); } catch (e) {}
  state = 'lobby'; $('#menu').hidden = true; $('#lobby').hidden = false; armBack();
  $('#lobbyCode').textContent = code; $('#lobbyLink').value = inviteLink();
  $('#lobbyNote').textContent = listed
    ? 'Your room is listed under Open rooms, so friends can join from there. They can also use the link or the code.'
    : 'This room is private. Your friend can open the link, or type the code into Join with code.';
  lobbyStatus('Connecting…'); updateLobbyWatch(); netConnect();
}
function leaveOnline() {
  if (!NET.on) return;
  netSend({ t: 'bye' });
  NET.on = false; NET.started = false; clearTimeout(NET.timer); freeSeat(); NET.cloth = null; NET.peerCue = 'house'; NET.peerGlove = 'none'; setCloth();
  const ws = NET.ws; NET.ws = null; if (ws) { try { ws.close(1000, 'bye'); } catch (e) {} }
  try { history.replaceState(null, '', location.href.split('#')[0]); } catch (e) {}
  $('#lobby').hidden = true; $('#thinking').hidden = true; updateNetBadge();
}
function showGameUI() {
  armBack();
  $('#menu').hidden = true; $('#over').hidden = true; $('#lobby').hidden = true; $('#hud').hidden = false; $('#practice').hidden = true;
  if (cam.mode === 'attract') { cam.mode = 'free'; cam.free.dist = 3.1 * Math.pow(P.L / 2.54, 0.9); }
  toastClear();
}
function hostStart() {
  if (M.mode === 'practice') M.mode = '8ball';
  applyTable(tableKeyFor());
  const balls = (M.mode === '9ball' ? C.rack9 : C.rack8)();
  const setup = { t: 'setup', mode: M.mode, guide: M.guide === 'auto' ? 'ghost' : M.guide,
    balls: balls.map(b => [b.id, b.x, b.z]), breaker: NET.games % 2, wins: matchDone() ? [0, 0] : [...matchWins], raceTo: M.race || 0, blackOne: !!M.blackOne };
  netSend(setup); startOnlineGame(setup);
}
function startOnlineGame(m) {
  if (!['8ball', '9ball', 'uk8'].includes(m.mode) || !Array.isArray(m.balls)) return;
  M.mode = m.mode; NET.guide = GUIDE_TXT[m.guide] ? m.guide : 'ghost';
  applyTable(tableKeyFor());

function netTick(now, dt) {

}
function updateNetBadge() {
  const el = $('#net');
  if (!NET.on) { el.textContent = ''; return; }
  const ok = NET.link === 'online' && NET.peer;
  el.textContent = NET.link !== 'online' ? 'Reconnecting to the relay…' : ok ? `${NET.peerName} is connected` : `${NET.peerName} is not connected`;
  el.dataset.ok = ok ? '1' : '0';
}
// quick chat: preset messages only, so there's nothing to moderate in listed rooms
const CHAT = ['Nice shot!', 'Unlucky', 'Good luck', 'Good game', 'Thanks!', 'Oops!', 'Wow!', 'One more?'];
let lastChat = 0;
function sendChat(i) {
  if (!NET.on || performance.now() - lastChat < 1200) return;
  lastChat = performance.now(); netSend({ t: 'chat', i }); toast(`You: ${CHAT[i]}`, 'chat'); $('#chatPop').hidden = true;
}
CHAT.forEach((txt, i) => { const b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'btn'; b.textContent = txt; b.addEventListener('click', () => sendChat(i)); $('#chatPop').appendChild(b); });
$('#bChat').addEventListener('click', () => { $('#chatPop').hidden = !$('#chatPop').hidden; });
function concedeFrame(fromRemote, loser) {
  if (game.over || M.mode === 'practice') return false;
  if (state === 'moving' || state === 'stroke' || state === 'botAim') { if (!fromRemote) toast('Wait for the balls to stop first', 'info'); return false; }
  if (loser !== 0 && loser !== 1) loser = NET.on ? NET.seat : (M.opp === 'bot' ? 0 : game.turn);
  const w = 1 - loser;
  game = { ...game, over: true, winner: w }; matchWins[w]++;
  state = 'over'; bot = null; $('#thinking').hidden = true; $('#offer').hidden = true; updateHUD();
  frameEarn(); if (CAR.on) careerFrameOver();
  showOver({ reason2: 'Conceded the frame' }, loser);
  return true;
}
$('#bConcede').addEventListener('click', () => { togglePause(false); if (concedeFrame(false) && NET.on) netSend({ t: 'concede' }); });
$('#bOfferRerack').addEventListener('click', () => {
  togglePause(false);
  if (!NET.on || game.over) return;
  netSend({ t: 'rerackOffer' }); toast(`Re-rack offered. Waiting for ${NET.peerName}`, 'info');
});
$('#bOfferYes').addEventListener('click', () => { $('#offer').hidden = true; netSend({ t: 'rerackYes' }); if (NET.seat === 0) hostStart(); });
$('#bOfferNo').addEventListener('click', () => { $('#offer').hidden = true; netSend({ t: 'rerackNo' }); });
function updateAgainBtn() {
  const b = $('#bAgain');
  const base = matchDone() ? 'New match' : raceTo() ? 'Next frame' : 'Play again';
  b.textContent = NET.again[NET.seat] ? `Waiting for ${NET.peerName}…` : NET.again[1 - NET.seat] ? `${base}: accept` : base;
}
function tryRematch() {
  if (!NET.on) return;
  if (NET.again[0] && NET.again[1]) { NET.again = [false, false]; if (NET.seat === 0) { NET.games++; hostStart(); } }
  else if (!$('#over').hidden) updateAgainBtn();
}
$('#bJoin').addEventListener('click', () => { ensureAudio(); sfx('ui'); startOnline(cleanCode($('#netCode').value)); });
// open rooms list: watched live from the relay while the online menu is showing
const LW = { ws: null, rooms: [], off: 0, retry: 0, timer: 0, want: false, failed: false, loaded: false };
const MODE_NAME = { '8ball': '8-ball', '9ball': '9-ball', uk8: 'Reds & yellows' };
function updateLobbyWatch() {
  const want = state === 'menu' && !NET.on && menuScreen() === 'online' && !!relayBase() && document.visibilityState === 'visible';
  LW.want = want;
  // short delay, so opening an invite link (which jumps straight into a room) never starts a list connection
  if (want && !LW.ws && !LW.pending) LW.pending = setTimeout(() => { LW.pending = 0; if (LW.want && !LW.ws) lobbyConnect(); }, 300);
  if (!want && LW.ws) {
    const ws = LW.ws; LW.ws = null;
    if (ws.readyState === 0) ws.onopen = () => { try { ws.close(1000); } catch (e) {} };
    else { try { ws.close(1000); } catch (e) {} }
  }
  renderRooms();
}
function lobbyConnect() {
  let ws;
  try { ws = new WebSocket(`${relayBase()}/lobby`); } catch (e) { LW.failed = true; renderRooms(); return; }
  LW.ws = ws; renderRooms();
  ws.onopen = () => { LW.retry = 0; LW.failed = false; renderRooms(); };
  ws.onmessage = e => {
    if (e.data === 'pong') return;
    let m; try { m = JSON.parse(e.data); } catch (err) { return; }
    if (m.t === 'rooms' && Array.isArray(m.rooms)) { LW.rooms = m.rooms; LW.off = (Number(m.now) || Date.now()) - Date.now(); LW.loaded = true; renderRooms(); }
  };
  ws.onclose = () => {
    if (LW.ws === ws) LW.ws = null;
    if (!LW.want) return;
    LW.failed = LW.retry > 1; renderRooms();
    clearTimeout(LW.timer); LW.timer = setTimeout(() => { if (LW.want && !LW.ws) lobbyConnect(); }, Math.min(10000, 1000 * Math.pow(2, LW.retry++)));
  };
}
setInterval(() => { if (LW.ws && LW.ws.readyState === 1) LW.ws.send('ping'); }, 25000);
setInterval(() => { if (LW.want) renderRooms(); }, 30000);
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { updateLobbyWatch(); if (!document.hidden && state === 'menu') updateRejoin(); });
function renderRooms() {
  const el = $('#roomList'), live = $('#roomsLive');
  const note = t => { el.innerHTML = ''; const d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'note'; d.textContent = t; el.appendChild(d); };
  if (!relayBase()) { live.textContent = ''; note('Add your relay address to config.js to see open rooms.'); return; }
  live.textContent = LW.ws && LW.ws.readyState === 1 ? 'Live' : LW.failed ? "Can't reach the relay" : 'Connecting…';
  if (!LW.rooms.length) { note(LW.loaded ? 'No open rooms right now. Create a listed room and it will show up here.' : 'Looking for open rooms…'); return; }
  el.innerHTML = '';
  const now = Date.now() + LW.off;
  for (const r of LW.rooms) {
    const row = document.createElement('div'); row.className = 'roomItem';
    const info = document.createElement('div');
    const n = document.createElement('div'); n.className = 'rn'; n.textContent = `${cleanName(r.name, 'Player')}'s room`;
    const mins = Math.max(0, Math.round((now - Number(r.at)) / 60000));
    const d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'rm'; d.textContent = `${MODE_NAME[r.mode] || '8-ball'}, ${mins < 1 ? 'just opened' : mins + ' min waiting'}`;
    info.append(n, d);
    const b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'btn'; b.textContent = 'Join';
    b.addEventListener('click', () => { ensureAudio(); sfx('ui'); startOnline(cleanCode(r.code)); });
    row.append(info, b); el.appendChild(row);
  }
}

$('#netCode').addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') $('#bJoin').click(); });
$('#bLobbyCancel').addEventListener('click', () => { sfx('ui'); clearRejoin(); toMenu(); });
$('#bCopyLink').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const link = inviteLink();
  try {
    if (navigator.share && matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches) { await navigator.share({ title: 'Retro Rack', text: 'Play pool with me', url: link }); return; }
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(link); lobbyStatus('Invite link copied. Send it to your friend.');
  } catch (e) { const i = $('#lobbyLink'); i.focus(); i.select(); try { document.execCommand('copy'); lobbyStatus('Invite link copied.'); } catch (e2) { lobbyStatus('Copy the link below and send it to your friend.'); } }
});
try { $('#netName').value = localStorage.getItem('retroRack.name') || ''; } catch (e) {}
// dev mode (see the settings section): checked as each letter is typed, so it's done before Create or Join is pressed
async function isDevWord(v) {
  if (!v || !(window.crypto && crypto.subtle)) return false;
  try { const h = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode('retroRack:' + v.trim().toLowerCase())); return [...new Uint8Array(h)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('') === DEVW.print; }
  catch (e) { return false; }
}
function setDev(on) {
  DEV = on; try { if (on) localStorage.setItem('retroRack.dev', '1'); else localStorage.removeItem('retroRack.dev'); } catch (e) {}
  LOCK = on ? devLocker() : lockerRead() || LK.newLocker();
  checkLooks(); saveS(); applyLook(); sendLook(); refreshMenus();
  toast(on ? 'Dev mode on: every look, £100,000 and 50 of each case, in a test locker' : 'Dev mode off: your own locker is back', 'good');
}
$('#netName').addEventListener('input', async () => {
  const v = $('#netName').value; if (!(await isDevWord(v)) || $('#netName').value !== v) return;
  try { $('#netName').value = localStorage.getItem('retroRack.name') || ''; } catch (e) { $('#netName').value = ''; }
  setDev(!DEV);
});
$('#sDev').addEventListener('click', () => { sfx('ui'); setDev(false); });

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ installable app
// Only on a real web address: a downloaded copy opened from disk can't install or use a service worker.
$('#ver').textContent = `Version ${BUILD}${DEV ? ' · DEV' : ''}`;
if (/^https?:$/.test(location.protocol)) {
  const link = document.createElement('link'); link.rel = 'manifest'; link.href = 'manifest.webmanifest';
  const icon = document.createElement('link'); icon.rel = 'apple-touch-icon'; icon.href = 'icons/icon-192.png';
  document.head.append(link, icon);
  if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) navigator.serviceWorker.register('sw.js').catch(() => { /* the game works without it */ });
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ phone and screen comforts
// Vibration: only Android lets web pages vibrate, so the setting only shows on touch screens that offer it.
const canVibrate = 'vibrate' in navigator && matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches;
function buzz(pattern) {
  if (!canVibrate || !S.vibrate || replay || TRK.demo) return;
  if (navigator.userActivation && !navigator.userActivation.hasBeenActive) return;   // refused (with a console warning) before the first tap
  try { navigator.vibrate(pattern); } catch (e) {}
}
$('#rowVibrate').hidden = !canVibrate;

// Fullscreen: iPhones don't allow it for web pages, so the buttons only show where the browser does
const canFull = !!(document.fullscreenEnabled && document.documentElement.requestFullscreen);
function toggleFull() {
  if (document.fullscreenElement) document.exitFullscreen().catch(() => {});
  else document.documentElement.requestFullscreen({ navigationUI: 'hide' }).catch(() => {});
}
// the menu's corner icons: drawn as pixel art from these rows (x = a pixel)
const ICONS = {
  gear: ['...xxxx...', '.x.xxxx.x.', '..xxxxxx..', 'xxxx..xxxx', 'xxx....xxx', 'xxx....xxx', 'xxxx..xxxx', '..xxxxxx..', '.x.xxxx.x.', '...xxxx...'],
  full: ['xxx....xxx', 'x........x', 'x........x', '..........', '..........', '..........', '..........', 'x........x', 'x........x', 'xxx....xxx'],
  unfull: ['..x....x..', '..x....x..', 'xxx....xxx', '..........', '..........', '..........', '..........', 'xxx....xxx', '..x....x..', '..x....x..'],
};
function setIcon(btn, name) {
  const rows = ICONS[name], cells = [];
  rows.forEach((r, y) => { for (let x = 0; x < r.length; x++) if (r[x] === 'x') cells.push(`<rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="1" height="1"/>`); });
  const old = btn.querySelector('svg'); if (old) old.remove();
  btn.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', `<svg viewBox="0 0 ${rows[0].length} ${rows.length}" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true">${cells.join('')}</svg>`);
}
setIcon($('#bMenuSettings'), 'gear');
function updateFullBtns() {
  const on = !!document.fullscreenElement, word = on ? 'Exit fullscreen' : 'Fullscreen';
  $('#bFull').hidden = !canFull; $('#bFull').textContent = word; $('#bFull').setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(on));
  const m = $('#bMenuFull'); m.hidden = !canFull; m.title = word; m.querySelector('.vh').textContent = word; m.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(on)); setIcon(m, on ? 'unfull' : 'full');
}
$('#bFull').addEventListener('click', () => { sfx('ui'); toggleFull(); });
$('#bMenuFull').addEventListener('click', () => { ensureAudio(); sfx('ui'); toggleFull(); });
document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', updateFullBtns);
updateFullBtns();

// "Turn your phone sideways": only on a phone held upright; goes when it's rotated, and OK hides it for good
const portraitPhone = matchMedia('(pointer: coarse) and (orientation: portrait) and (max-width: 600px)');
let rotateOff = false;
try { rotateOff = localStorage.getItem('retroRack.rotateHint') === 'off'; } catch (e) {}
function updateRotateHint() { $('#rotate').hidden = rotateOff || !portraitPhone.matches; }
portraitPhone.addEventListener('change', updateRotateHint);
$('#bRotateOk').addEventListener('click', () => { rotateOff = true; try { localStorage.setItem('retroRack.rotateHint', 'off'); } catch (e) {} updateRotateHint(); });
updateRotateHint();

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ main loop
let lastT = performance.now();
function frame(now) {
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  const dt = Math.min(0.05, (now - lastT) / 1000); lastT = now;
  if (!paused) {
    handleKeys(dt);
    if (state === 'moving') physicsTick(dt);
    else if (state === 'botThink') botThinkTick(now);
    else if (state === 'botAim') botAimTick(dt);
    potAnimTick(dt);
  }
  netTick(now, dt);
  updateBalls();
  updateCue(paused ? 0 : dt);
  if (aimDirty) updateAimTxt();
  updateGuides(now);
  updateMarkers(now);
  if (state !== 'aim') { G_CUE.visible = G_CUE2.visible = G_OBJ.visible = G_GHOST.visible = false; }
  cam.update(dt);
  renderer.setRenderTarget(rt); renderer.render(scene, camera);
  renderer.setRenderTarget(null); renderer.render(postScene, postCam);
}
addEventListener('resize', resize);
resize(); applyLook(); syncBallMeshes(); refreshMenus(); updateCamButtons();
requestAnimationFrame(frame);
{ // opening an invite link joins the room straight away
  const m = /#room=([A-Za-z0-9]+)/.exec(location.hash);
  if (m) { const code = cleanCode(m[1]); $('#netCode').value = code; M.opp = 'online'; if (M.mode === 'practice') M.mode = '8ball'; refreshMenus(); if (relayBase()) startOnline(code); else { menuReset(['home', 'multi', 'online']); menuNote('This is an invite link, but online play needs the relay address in config.js first.'); } }
}
window.__rr = { get state() { return state; }, get world() { return world; }, get game() { return game; }, NET, get replay() { return replay; },
  get matchWins() { return matchWins; }, CAR, LK, DEVW, get dev() { return DEV; }, concedeFrame, cheer, applyLook, get cheering() { return cheerGlove.visible ? CHEER.kind : ''; }, get lock() { return LOCK; }, get earn() { return EARN; }, get reel() { return REEL; },
  get look() { return { venue: VEN.key, sign: VEN.sign, cue: cueNow, cloth: NET.on && NET.cloth ? NET.cloth : S.cloth, glove: gloveNow, clothTex: !!clothMat.map }; },
  ballScreen(id) { const b = world.balls.find(x => x.id === id); const v = new THREE.Vector3(b.x, R, b.z).project(camera); const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect(); return [r.left + (v.x + 1) / 2 * r.width, r.top + (1 - v.y) / 2 * r.height]; }, aim, cam, startGame, M, S, beginStroke, toggleTop, toggleAimCam,
  marked() { return MK.map((k, id) => k.visible ? id : -1).filter(id => id >= 0); } };
})();
```

## src/shell.html

Page markup and CSS. The build fills in the fonts, favicon and code (__PS2P__, __VT323__, __FAVICON__, __BUNDLE__). (525 lines)

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en-GB">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<script>window.RETRO_RACK_RELAY = "";</script>
<!-- Online play: the relay address lives in config.js, next to this file. -->
<script src="config.js"></script>
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover">
<title>Retro Rack: 3D pixel pool</title>
<meta name="theme-color" content="#1a1433">
<link rel="icon" type="image/png" href="data:image/png;base64,__FAVICON__">
<style>
@font-face{font-family:'Press Start 2P';src:url(data:font/woff2;base64,__PS2P__) format('woff2');font-display:block}
@font-face{font-family:'VT323';src:url(data:font/woff2;base64,__VT323__) format('woff2');font-display:block}
:root{--night:#1a1433;--plum:#3a2452;--plum2:#4e3270;--lamp:#ffc56b;--baize:#1d8a74;--chalk:#6cb8ff;--rose:#ff6f8f;--paper:#f7ead2;--ink:#0d0a1c}
*{box-sizing:border-box}
html,body{margin:0;height:100%;overflow:hidden;background:var(--night);color:var(--paper);font-family:'VT323',ui-monospace,monospace;font-size:22px;line-height:1.15;-webkit-user-select:none;user-select:none;touch-action:none;-webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent}
[hidden]{display:none!important}
#view{position:fixed;left:0;top:0;display:block;image-rendering:pixelated;image-rendering:crisp-edges;cursor:crosshair}
#scan{position:fixed;inset:0;pointer-events:none;display:none;background:repeating-linear-gradient(to bottom,rgba(0,0,0,.2) 0 1px,transparent 1px 3px)}
body.scan #scan{display:block}
.panel{background:rgba(22,17,44,.9);border:3px solid var(--lamp);box-shadow:6px 6px 0 var(--ink)}
.btn{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:10px;line-height:1.35;color:var(--paper);background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink);border-radius:0;box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35),inset 3px 3px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.1);padding:9px 11px;cursor:pointer}
.btn:hover{background:var(--plum2)}
.btn:active{transform:translate(2px,2px)}
.btn[aria-pressed="true"]{background:var(--lamp);color:var(--ink)}
.btn:disabled{opacity:.45;cursor:default;transform:none}
button:focus-visible{outline:3px dashed var(--chalk);outline-offset:2px}
kbd{font-family:'VT323',monospace;font-size:19px;line-height:1;background:var(--plum);border:2px solid var(--ink);padding:0 5px;margin:0 1px;color:var(--paper);box-shadow:inset -2px -2px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35)}

/* shared by the menus and the panels (settings, game over, waiting room, offers) */
.row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:104px 1fr;gap:6px 14px;align-items:start;margin:0 0 12px}
.lbl{color:var(--lamp);font-size:22px;padding-top:6px}
.seg{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px}
.note{grid-column:2;font-size:20px;opacity:.82;max-width:46ch}
.swatches{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.swatch{width:34px;height:34px;border:3px solid var(--ink);border-radius:0;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.3)}
.swatch[aria-pressed="true"]{outline:3px solid var(--lamp);outline-offset:2px}
.actions{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:20px}
/* menu: a stack of screens. Moving between them, the whole panel glides across the screen: the old one
   leaves one side as the new one arrives from the other, like slides on a strip (see menuSlide in game.js) */
#menu{position:fixed;inset:0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:flex-start;padding:3vh 4vw;pointer-events:none;overflow:hidden}
#menuPanel{pointer-events:auto;width:min(660px,94vw);max-height:94vh;display:flex;flex-direction:column;overflow:hidden;padding:0}
.mGhost{position:fixed;z-index:3;pointer-events:none;margin:0}
.mTop{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;padding:14px 20px 0;flex:none}
#mTitle{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:12px;line-height:1.4;color:var(--lamp);margin:0}
#mStage{display:grid;overflow:auto;min-height:0;padding:16px 20px 14px}
.mScreen{grid-area:1 / 1;min-width:0}
.title{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:clamp(26px,4.2vw,42px);line-height:1.15;color:var(--lamp);margin:0 0 16px;text-shadow:3px 3px 0 var(--rose),6px 6px 0 var(--plum2),9px 9px 0 var(--ink)}
.cards{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(250px,1fr));gap:10px}
.card{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;text-align:left;padding:10px 12px;font:inherit;color:var(--paper);background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink);border-radius:0;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35),inset 3px 3px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.1),4px 4px 0 var(--ink);transition:transform .12s,background .12s,border-color .12s}
.card:hover{background:var(--plum2);border-color:var(--lamp);transform:translate(-1px,-2px)}
.card:active{transform:translate(2px,2px);box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35),inset 3px 3px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.1),2px 2px 0 var(--ink)}
.card[aria-pressed="true"]{border-color:var(--lamp)}
.cardArt{height:32px;width:auto;image-rendering:pixelated;flex:none}
.cardBody{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;min-width:0}
.cardName{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:11px;line-height:1.3;color:var(--lamp);white-space:nowrap}
.cardTxt{font-size:18px;line-height:1;opacity:.72}
.cardTxt:empty{display:none}
#bStart,#bQuick,#bCreate,#bCareer{font-size:12px;padding:12px 16px;background:var(--rose);color:var(--ink)}
#bStart:hover,#bQuick:hover,#bCreate:hover,#bCareer:hover{background:#ff8ba5}
.quickRow{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px}.quickRow>.btn{margin-bottom:12px;text-align:left}
#bRejoin{font-size:12px;padding:12px 16px;background:var(--lamp);color:var(--ink)}#bRejoin:hover{background:#ffd48e}
.mFoot{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;flex:none;padding:10px 20px 14px;border-top:3px solid var(--plum2)}
/* Settings and Fullscreen: small icons in the top-right corner while in the menus (inside the panel's corner on phones) */
#menuIcons{position:fixed;top:14px;right:16px;display:flex;gap:8px;pointer-events:auto;z-index:2}
.iconBtn{padding:7px;line-height:0}.iconBtn svg{width:22px;height:22px;shape-rendering:crispEdges}
.vh{position:absolute;width:1px;height:1px;overflow:hidden;clip:rect(0 0 0 0);white-space:nowrap}
#ver{margin:12px 0 0;font-size:16px;opacity:.5}
/* career: the new-career form, the hub (you and your events) and an event (the draw and your next opponent) */
.portrait,.shopSw{image-rendering:pixelated;flex:none}
#cName{width:200px}
.cStyleBtn{padding:4px 6px;line-height:0}.cStyleBtn[aria-pressed="true"]{border-color:var(--lamp);background:var(--plum2)}
.cCols{gap:6px;align-items:center}.cCols .swatches{gap:5px;margin-right:10px}.cCols .swatch{width:24px;height:24px}.cColLbl{font-size:18px;opacity:.8}
.cTop{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:0 0 10px}
.cMe{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px}
.cMeName{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:12px;color:var(--lamp);margin-bottom:6px}
.cMeStats{font-size:20px}
.cEvents{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px}
.cTiers{margin:0 0 8px}.cTiers .btn.locked{opacity:.55}
.shopGrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(190px,1fr));gap:7px;margin-top:10px}
.shopItem{display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto 1fr;grid-template-rows:auto auto;gap:2px 10px;align-items:center;text-align:left;padding:6px 9px;font:inherit;color:var(--paper);background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink);border-radius:0;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35),inset 3px 3px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.1),3px 3px 0 var(--ink)}
.shopItem:hover{background:var(--plum2)}.shopItem[aria-pressed="true"]{border-color:var(--lamp)}
.shopItem>img,.shopItem>.shopSw{grid-row:1 / span 2}.shopSw{width:30px;height:30px;border:3px solid var(--ink)}
.shopName{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:9px}.shopState{font-size:18px;color:var(--lamp)}
.shopName{white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;min-width:0}.shopItem.locked{opacity:.62}.shopItem.locked:hover{opacity:.85}
/* the locker: cases, then pages of looks */
.lockTop{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px 12px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:8px}.lockTop .cInfo{margin:0;flex:1 1 auto}
#lockBody.shopGrid{margin-top:0;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr))}
.caseGrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr));gap:8px}
.caseCard{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:4px;padding:8px 6px;text-align:center;background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink);box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35),inset 3px 3px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.1),3px 3px 0 var(--ink)}
.caseName{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:9px;line-height:1.3}.caseN{font-size:18px;color:var(--lamp)}.caseOdds{font-size:15px;line-height:1;opacity:.8}
.caseBtns{display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:center;margin-top:auto}.caseBtns .btn{font-size:8px;padding:6px 7px}.caseBtns .btn.dim{opacity:.5}
.lockFoot{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;margin-top:8px;font-size:17px}.lockFoot>span:first-child{margin-right:auto;opacity:.85}.lockFoot .btn{font-size:9px;padding:5px 9px}
#lockNote{grid-column:auto;margin:6px 0 0}#lockNote:empty{display:none}
.picker{align-items:center;flex-wrap:nowrap}.pickCur{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;min-width:170px;font-size:19px}.pickCur .shopSw{width:22px;height:22px;border-width:2px}
/* opening a case: a reel of the case's items glides past a marker and stops on the prize */
.reelPanel{width:min(560px,94vw);text-align:center;padding:16px 18px}
#reelWin{position:relative;overflow:hidden;height:72px;margin:10px 0;border:3px solid var(--ink);background:#0d0a1c;cursor:pointer}
#reelStrip{display:flex;gap:6px;padding:6px 0;will-change:transform}
.reelTile{flex:none;width:58px;height:54px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:var(--plum);border:3px solid}
.reelTile.won{background:var(--plum2);outline:3px solid var(--lamp)}
.reelMark{position:absolute;top:0;bottom:0;left:50%;width:2px;margin-left:-1px;background:var(--lamp);box-shadow:0 0 6px var(--lamp)}
#reelTxt{min-height:1.3em;margin:0 0 6px;font-size:20px}.reelRarity{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:10px}
.cTier{font-size:19px;color:var(--lamp);margin:0}
.cEvt{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr auto;gap:2px 14px;align-items:center;text-align:left;padding:7px 11px;font:inherit;color:var(--paper);background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink);border-radius:0;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:inset -3px -3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.35),inset 3px 3px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.1),3px 3px 0 var(--ink)}
.cEvt:hover:not([aria-disabled="true"]){background:var(--plum2);border-color:var(--lamp)}
.cEvt[aria-disabled="true"]{opacity:.55;cursor:default}
.cEvtName{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:10px;line-height:1.4}
.cEvtTxt{font-size:18px;opacity:.85}
.cEvtState{grid-row:1 / span 2;grid-column:2;font-size:19px;color:var(--lamp);text-align:right}
#cNote{grid-column:auto;margin:8px 0 0}#cNote:empty{display:none}
.cTools{display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-left:auto}.cTools .btn{font-size:8px;padding:7px 9px}
.stars{image-rendering:pixelated;flex:none}
.cInfo{margin:0 0 8px;font-size:19px}
.cEv{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,3fr) minmax(0,2fr);gap:14px;align-items:start}
.bracket{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr));gap:5px;font-size:17px}
.bracket.bField{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr))}
.bCol{display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:space-around;gap:3px;min-height:100%}
.bName{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:4px;padding:1px 5px;background:rgba(13,10,28,.6);border-left:3px solid var(--plum2);white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden}
.bName span:first-child{overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis}
.bName.you{color:var(--lamp);border-left-color:var(--lamp)}.bName.out{opacity:.45}.bName.champ{border-left-color:var(--lamp);color:var(--lamp)}
.cOpp{display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto 1fr;gap:4px 10px;align-content:start;font-size:18px}
.cOpp .portrait{grid-row:1 / span 3}
.cOppName{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:9px;line-height:1.5;color:var(--lamp)}
.cOppTxt{grid-column:1 / -1;opacity:.9}
@media (max-width:640px){.cEv{grid-template-columns:1fr}}
#sc-setup .row{grid-template-columns:96px 1fr;margin-bottom:10px}#sc-setup .note{grid-column:2;font-size:18px;opacity:.72}
@media (max-width:500px){#sc-setup .row{grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:4px}#sc-setup .note{grid-column:1}#lockBody.shopGrid,.caseGrid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr))}}   /* narrow upright phones: labels above, so the buttons wrap less */
/* short screens (a phone on its side): everything on one screen, no scrolling */
@media (max-height:500px){
  #menu{padding:2vh 3vw}
  .title{font-size:22px;margin-bottom:10px}
  #mStage{padding:10px 14px 8px}.mTop{padding:8px 14px 0}.mFoot{padding:6px 14px 8px}
  .cards{grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(200px,1fr));gap:7px}
  .card{padding:6px 9px;gap:9px}.cardArt{height:26px}.cardName{font-size:9px}.cardTxt{font-size:16px}.cardBody{gap:4px}
  #bStart,#bQuick,#bCreate,#bRejoin,#bCareer{font-size:10px;padding:9px 12px}.quickRow>.btn{margin-bottom:8px}
  #sc-cnew .row{grid-template-columns:78px 1fr;gap:3px 8px;margin-bottom:6px}#sc-cnew .lbl{font-size:18px;padding-top:3px}#sc-cnew .note{display:none}
  #cName{font-size:18px;padding:2px 6px}.cStyleBtn{padding:2px 4px}.cCols .swatch{width:20px;height:20px}
  .cTop{margin-bottom:6px}.cMeName{font-size:10px;margin-bottom:3px}.cMeStats{font-size:17px}.cEvents{gap:4px}
  .cEvt{grid-template-columns:auto 1fr auto;padding:4px 9px}.cEvtName{font-size:8px}.cEvtTxt,.cEvtState,.cTier{font-size:16px}
  .cEvtState{grid-row:1;grid-column:3}.cEvtTxt{grid-row:1;grid-column:2;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis}.cColLbl{font-size:16px}
  .cInfo{font-size:16px;margin-bottom:4px}.bracket{font-size:15px;gap:3px}.cOpp{font-size:16px}.cOppName{font-size:8px}
  .cTiers{margin-bottom:5px}.shopGrid{gap:5px;margin-top:6px;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr))}.shopItem{padding:4px 7px}.shopName{font-size:8px}.shopState{font-size:16px}.shopSw{width:24px;height:24px}
  .lockTop{margin-bottom:5px}.lockTop .cInfo{font-size:16px}.shopItem>img.portrait{max-height:24px;width:auto}.caseGrid{gap:5px}.caseCard{padding:4px 4px;gap:2px}
  .caseCard>img{width:36px;height:28px}.caseName{font-size:8px}.caseN{font-size:16px}.caseOdds{font-size:14px}.caseBtns .btn{padding:5px 5px}
  .lockFoot{margin-top:5px;font-size:15px}.lockFoot .btn{padding:4px 8px}#lockNote{margin-top:3px;font-size:16px}
  #reelWin{height:64px;margin:6px 0}.reelTile{width:52px;height:48px}.reelPanel{padding:10px 14px}
  #ver{margin-top:6px;font-size:15px}
  #sc-setup .row{grid-template-columns:78px 1fr;gap:3px 8px;margin-bottom:6px}#sc-setup .lbl{font-size:18px;padding-top:3px}
  #sc-setup .note{display:none}#menu .seg{gap:4px}#menu .seg .btn{font-size:8px;padding:6px 7px}
  #sc-online .onl{gap:5px}#roomList{max-height:78px}#netName,#netCode{font-size:18px;padding:2px 6px}
  .mFoot .btn{font-size:8px;padding:7px 9px}
  #menuIcons{top:calc(2vh + 7px);right:calc(3vw + 9px);gap:6px}.iconBtn{padding:5px}.iconBtn svg{width:16px;height:16px}
}


/* in-game hud */
#board{position:fixed;top:8px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:stretch;pointer-events:none}
.pl,#mid{padding:6px 10px;background:rgba(22,17,44,.86);border:3px solid var(--plum2);box-shadow:5px 5px 0 var(--ink)}
.pl{min-width:158px}
.pl.active{border-color:var(--lamp)}
.pname{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:8px;margin-bottom:5px;white-space:nowrap}
.pl.active .pname::before{content:'\25B6  ';color:var(--lamp)}
.pgroup{font-size:17px;opacity:.85}
.pballs,#rack{display:flex;gap:3px;margin-top:4px;min-height:14px}
#rack{justify-content:center}
#rack:empty{display:none}
.bi{width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:50%;border:2px solid var(--ink);display:inline-block}
.bi.gone{opacity:.2}
.bi.ring{outline:2px solid var(--lamp);outline-offset:1px}
#mid{text-align:center;min-width:150px}
#modeName{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:8px;color:var(--lamp)}
#turnLine{font-size:19px;margin-top:4px}
#extra{font-size:17px;color:var(--chalk);margin-top:2px}
#extra:empty{display:none}
#score{font-size:16px;margin-top:3px;opacity:.8}
#score:empty{display:none}
#notices{position:fixed;top:calc(var(--bb,112px) + 10px);left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:6px;pointer-events:none;width:max-content;max-width:94vw;z-index:5}
.toast{padding:5px 12px;font-size:20px;background:var(--ink);border:3px solid var(--lamp);opacity:0;transform:translateY(-6px);transition:opacity .2s,transform .2s;text-align:center;max-width:94vw}
.toast.show{opacity:1;transform:none}
.toast[data-kind=foul]{border-color:var(--rose);color:#ffd8e0}
.toast[data-kind=good]{border-color:var(--chalk)}
#thinking{font-size:19px;padding:5px 10px;background:rgba(13,10,28,.8);border:3px solid var(--plum2)}
#thinking::after{content:'...';display:inline-block;width:1.4em;text-align:left;animation:dots 1.2s steps(4) infinite;overflow:hidden;vertical-align:bottom}
@keyframes dots{0%{width:0}100%{width:1.4em}}
#tools{position:fixed;top:8px;right:8px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;align-items:flex-end}
#practice{position:fixed;top:8px;left:8px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;align-items:flex-start}
#shot{position:fixed;right:10px;bottom:10px;display:grid;grid-template-columns:110px 64px;gap:8px 10px;padding:9px 10px;align-items:start;transition:opacity .2s}
#shot.dim{opacity:.5}
.spinWrap,.powWrap{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:6px}
#spin{width:96px;height:96px;image-rendering:pixelated;cursor:crosshair;touch-action:none}
#spinTxt{font-size:17px;line-height:1.05;height:2.1em;overflow:hidden;white-space:pre-line;text-align:center;width:110px}
#pow{position:relative;width:24px;height:122px;background:var(--ink);border:3px solid var(--plum2);cursor:ns-resize;touch-action:none}
#powFill{position:absolute;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;background:linear-gradient(to top,var(--chalk) 0 33%,var(--lamp) 33% 70%,var(--rose) 70% 100%);background-size:100% 116px;background-position:bottom}
#powTxt{font-size:17px;white-space:nowrap;width:64px;text-align:center;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
#aimTxt{grid-column:1 / 3;text-align:center;font-size:17px;margin-bottom:-4px;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
#bShoot{grid-column:1 / 3;font-size:10px;padding:10px;background:var(--lamp);color:var(--ink)}
#bShoot:hover{background:#ffd48e}
#bihTip{font-size:19px;text-align:center;padding:5px 10px;background:rgba(13,10,28,.85);border:3px solid #7dffb0}
#help{position:fixed;left:10px;bottom:10px;width:min(400px,92vw);max-height:80vh;overflow:auto;padding:12px 14px 10px;font-size:18px}
#help h2,#pause h2{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:12px;color:var(--lamp);margin:0 0 10px}
#help dl{display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto 1fr;gap:6px 12px;margin:0 0 12px}
#help dt{color:var(--lamp)}
#help dd{margin:0}
#help p{margin:0 0 8px;max-width:48ch}
#bHelpClose{margin-top:4px}

/* overlays */
.overlay{position:fixed;inset:0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:rgba(13,10,28,.35);padding:4vh 4vw}
/* Settings: a big panel in four sections, two columns side by side where there's room; click outside it to close */
#pause{z-index:7}   /* above the game's messages */
#pausePanel{width:min(1060px,94vw);max-height:92vh;overflow:auto;padding:22px 30px}
#pause h2{font-size:15px;margin-bottom:12px}
.sGrid{columns:2;column-gap:44px}.sSec{break-inside:avoid;padding-bottom:4px}
.sSec h3{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:11px;color:var(--paper);opacity:.75;margin:2px 0 12px;padding-bottom:7px;border-bottom:3px solid var(--plum)}
#pause .row{grid-template-columns:118px 1fr;gap:4px 12px;margin-bottom:10px}#pause .lbl{font-size:25px;padding-top:7px}#pause .note{font-size:18px;max-width:none}
#pause .btn{font-size:11px;padding:10px 12px}#pause .seg{gap:7px}#pause .pickCur{font-size:22px}#pause .actions{margin-top:8px}
@media (max-width:880px){.sGrid{columns:1}}
#overPanel{width:min(460px,92vw);padding:24px 24px 20px;text-align:left}
#overTitle{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:26px;color:var(--lamp);margin:0 0 14px;text-shadow:3px 3px 0 var(--rose),6px 6px 0 var(--ink)}
#overWhy{font-size:24px;margin:0 0 6px}
#overStats{font-size:20px;opacity:.8;margin:0}
#overEarn{font-size:20px;color:var(--lamp);margin:6px 0 0}#overEarn:empty{display:none}

/* phones: narrow screens, and short ones such as a phone on its side */
@media (max-width:760px),(max-height:500px){
  html,body{font-size:19px}
  .row{grid-template-columns:1fr}.note{grid-column:1}
  #pause{padding:2vh 2vw}#pausePanel{width:96vw;max-height:96vh;padding:14px 16px}#pause .row{grid-template-columns:1fr;margin-bottom:12px}
  #pause .lbl{font-size:21px;padding-top:0}#pause .note{font-size:18px}#pause .btn{font-size:11px;padding:11px 13px;min-height:42px}
  #board{top:6px;gap:5px;transform:translateX(-50%) scale(.82);transform-origin:top center}
  .pl{min-width:130px}
  #shot{right:6px;bottom:6px;padding:7px;gap:5px 6px}
  #shot{grid-template-columns:84px 56px}
  #spin{width:64px;height:64px}#spinTxt{width:84px;font-size:15px}#powTxt{width:56px;font-size:15px}#aimTxt{font-size:15px}
  #pow{height:92px}#powFill{background-size:100% 86px}
  .toast{font-size:18px}#thinking,#bihTip{font-size:17px}
  #tools{top:auto;bottom:8px;right:auto;left:8px;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;max-width:44vw}
  #tools .btn,#practice .btn{font-size:8px;padding:7px 6px}
  #practice{top:auto;bottom:calc(var(--tb,120px) + 6px);display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;align-items:start;max-width:44vw}
  #help{bottom:auto;top:8px;max-height:70vh}
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.toast,#shot,.card{transition:none}.card:hover{transform:none}#thinking::after{animation:none;width:1.4em}}
#hud #tools .btn,#hud #practice .btn{font-size:8px;padding:7px 9px}
input{font-family:'VT323',monospace;font-size:21px;color:var(--paper);background:var(--ink);border:3px solid var(--plum2);border-radius:0;padding:5px 8px;-webkit-user-select:text;user-select:text;outline:none}
input:focus{border-color:var(--chalk)}
.onl{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px}
.onl .note{grid-column:auto}
.field{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;font-size:20px}
#netName{width:170px}#netCode{width:140px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:2px}
.note.warn{color:var(--rose)}.note.warn:empty{display:none}
#lobbyPanel{width:min(460px,92vw);padding:20px 22px}
#lobbyPanel h2{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-weight:400;font-size:12px;color:var(--lamp);margin:0 0 14px}
#lobbyCode{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:30px;letter-spacing:8px;color:var(--lamp);margin:6px 0 12px;text-shadow:3px 3px 0 var(--rose),5px 5px 0 var(--ink)}
#lobbyStatus{font-size:22px;margin:0 0 10px}
#lobbyLink{width:100%;font-size:17px}
#lobbyPanel .note{grid-column:auto;margin:12px 0 0}
#net{font-size:16px;margin-top:3px}#net:empty{display:none}
#net::before{content:'\25CF  ';color:#7dffb0}#net[data-ok="0"]::before{color:var(--rose)}
/* open rooms */
.roomsHead{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:baseline;font-size:20px;color:var(--lamp);margin-top:4px}
#roomsLive{font-size:17px;color:var(--chalk)}
#roomList{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;max-height:172px;overflow:auto;padding:6px;background:rgba(13,10,28,.55);border:3px solid var(--plum2)}
#roomList .note{margin:2px 4px}
.roomItem{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;padding:5px 8px;background:var(--plum);border:3px solid var(--ink)}
.roomItem .rn{font-size:20px}.roomItem .rm{font-size:17px;opacity:.8}
.roomItem .btn{flex:none}
#chatPop{position:fixed;top:8px;right:118px;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:6px;padding:8px;z-index:6}
.toast[data-kind=chat]{border-color:#7dffb0}
#aimRow{grid-column:1 / 3;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:6px}
#aimRow .btn{padding:8px 12px;font-size:10px;touch-action:none}
#trickInfo{padding:8px 10px;max-width:250px;font-size:17px}
#trickTitle{font-family:'Press Start 2P',monospace;font-size:8px;line-height:1.5;color:var(--lamp);margin-bottom:4px}
#trickGoal{margin-bottom:6px}
#trickInfo .btn{font-size:8px;padding:7px 8px}
#replayBar{position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:12px;transform:translateX(-50%);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:7px 10px;font-size:19px;z-index:6}
#ballPick{position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:10px;transform:translateX(-50%);width:min(300px,60vw);padding:10px 12px 12px;z-index:6}
#ballPick p{margin:0 0 8px;font-size:18px;line-height:1.1}
#ballPick .actions{margin-top:10px}
#ballGrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(8,1fr);gap:4px}
.bp{padding:3px;background:var(--ink);border:2px solid var(--plum2);cursor:pointer;line-height:0;opacity:.45}
.bp canvas{width:100%;height:auto;image-rendering:pixelated}
.bp[aria-pressed="true"]{opacity:1;border-color:var(--lamp)}
@media (max-width:760px) and (orientation:portrait){#ballPick{bottom:auto;top:calc(var(--bb,90px) + 10px);width:min(300px,90vw)}}   /* upright phones: no room at the bottom */
@media (max-height:500px){#ballPick{bottom:6px;padding:6px 8px 8px;left:calc(var(--lw,40vw) + 8px);right:calc(var(--sw,180px) + 8px);width:auto;transform:none}#ballGrid{grid-template-columns:repeat(5,1fr)}#ballPick p{font-size:15px;margin-bottom:5px}#ballPick .actions{margin-top:6px}#ballPick .btn{font-size:8px;padding:6px 8px}}
#rotate{position:fixed;left:50%;top:50%;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);width:min(300px,86vw);padding:14px 16px 16px;text-align:center;z-index:8}
#rotate p{margin:0 0 12px;font-size:22px}
.offerPanel{width:min(420px,92vw);padding:18px 20px}.offerPanel p{margin:0 0 6px;font-size:22px}
@media (max-width:760px),(max-height:500px){#chatPop{top:auto;bottom:calc(var(--tb,120px) + 6px);right:auto;left:8px}#replayBar{bottom:auto;top:calc(var(--bb,90px) + 10px)}#trickInfo{grid-column:1 / 3;max-width:none}#aimRow .btn{padding:7px 9px}}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<canvas id="view" aria-label="3D pool table"></canvas>
<div id="scan"></div>

<div id="hud" hidden>
  <div id="board">
    <div class="pl" id="p0"><div class="pname"></div><div class="pgroup"></div><div class="pballs"></div></div>
    <div id="mid"><div id="modeName"></div><div id="turnLine"></div><div id="extra"></div><div id="net"></div><div id="rack"></div><div id="score"></div></div>
    <div class="pl" id="p1"><div class="pname"></div><div class="pgroup"></div><div class="pballs"></div></div>
  </div>
  <div id="notices" role="status" aria-live="polite">
    <div id="thinking" hidden>CPU is lining up a shot</div>
    <div id="bihTip" hidden>Ball in hand: drag the cue ball to place it</div>
  </div>
  <div id="tools">
    <button class="btn" id="bCam" aria-pressed="false" title="Cue view (C)">Cue view</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bTop" aria-pressed="false" title="Overhead view (T)">Overhead</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bHelp" title="Controls (H)">Controls</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bPause" title="Pause (Esc)">Pause</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bFull" aria-pressed="false" hidden>Fullscreen</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bReplay" title="Replay the last shot (V)" hidden>Replay</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bChat" title="Quick chat" hidden>Chat</button>
  </div>
  <div id="chatPop" class="panel" hidden></div>
  <div id="practice" hidden>
    <button class="btn" id="bRerack" title="R">Rerack</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bUndo" title="U">Undo shot</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bHand" aria-pressed="false" title="B">Ball in hand</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bMove" aria-pressed="false">Edit table</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bBalls" aria-pressed="false" hidden>Add balls</button>
    <button class="btn" id="bSave">Save layout</button>
    <div id="trickInfo" class="panel" hidden>
      <div id="trickTitle"></div><div id="trickGoal"></div>
      <div class="seg"><button class="btn" id="bTrickPrev" aria-label="Previous trick">&lt;</button><button class="btn" id="bTrickNext" aria-label="Next trick">&gt;</button><button class="btn" id="bShowMe">Show me</button><button class="btn" id="bDelLayout">Delete</button></div>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div id="shot" class="panel">
    <div class="spinWrap"><canvas id="spin" width="32" height="32" aria-label="Cue tip position: drag to add spin"></canvas><div id="spinTxt">Centre ball</div></div>
    <div class="powWrap"><div id="pow" aria-label="Power: drag to set"><div id="powFill"></div></div><div id="powTxt">0 m/s</div></div>
    <div id="aimRow"><button class="btn" id="bAimL" aria-label="Turn aim left">&lt;</button><div id="aimTxt">Aim 0.00°</div><button class="btn" id="bAimR" aria-label="Turn aim right">&gt;</button></div>
    <button class="btn" id="bShoot">Shoot</button>
  </div>
  <div id="help" class="panel" hidden>
    <h2>Controls</h2>
    <dl>
      <dt>Aim</dt><dd>Drag across the table and the cue points at your cursor. Double-click to aim at a spot.</dd>
      <dt>Fine aim</dt><dd><kbd>←</kbd><kbd>→</kbd> or <kbd>A</kbd><kbd>D</kbd> turn 0.05° per press (holding does nothing more). Hold <kbd>Shift</kbd> with them to turn freely.</dd>
      <dt>Power</dt><dd><kbd>↑</kbd><kbd>↓</kbd> or <kbd>W</kbd><kbd>S</kbd>, or drag the bar.</dd>
      <dt>Spin</dt><dd>Drag on the cue ball, or <kbd>I</kbd><kbd>J</kbd><kbd>K</kbd><kbd>L</kbd>. <kbd>O</kbd> resets.</dd>
      <dt>Shoot</dt><dd><kbd>Space</kbd> or the Shoot button.</dd>
      <dt>Camera</dt><dd>Right-drag (or <kbd>Alt</kbd>-drag) to orbit, wheel to zoom, middle-drag to pan, <kbd>Q</kbd><kbd>E</kbd> to turn.</dd>
      <dt>Views</dt><dd><kbd>C</kbd> cue view, <kbd>T</kbd> overhead, <kbd>F</kbd> reset.</dd>
      <dt>Touch</dt><dd>One finger aims. Two fingers orbit and pinch to zoom.</dd>
      <dt>Replay</dt><dd><kbd>V</kbd> or the Replay button watches the last shot again, with slow motion. Orbit the camera while it plays.</dd>
      <dt>Practice</dt><dd><kbd>R</kbd> rerack, <kbd>U</kbd> undo, <kbd>B</kbd> ball in hand. Edit table lets you drag any ball and add or remove balls; Save layout keeps it under Trick shots.</dd>
    </dl>
    <p>8-ball: pot your group, then the 8. A scratch, hitting the wrong ball first, or no ball reaching a rail after contact is a foul and gives your opponent ball in hand.</p>
    <p>9-ball: always hit the lowest ball first. Pot the 9 on a legal shot to win.</p>
    <p>Reds &amp; yellows (British pub rules): pot your colour, then the black. Fouls are an in-off, missing every ball, hitting the wrong colour or the black first, or potting your opponent's colour. After a foul your opponent gets two visits, and the first is a free ball: they may hit any ball first. An in-off also gives ball in hand behind the baulk line. Potting the black early, or with a foul, loses the frame.</p>
    <p>Side spin makes the cue ball squirt slightly off line. The Full and Ghost + line guides allow for it; the others don't.</p>
    <button class="btn" id="bHelpClose">Close</button>
  </div>
</div>

<div id="menu">
  <div id="menuPanel" class="panel">
    <div class="mTop" id="mTop" hidden><button class="btn" id="mBack" type="button" aria-label="Back">&lt; Back</button><h2 id="mTitle"></h2></div>
    <div id="mStage">
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-home">
        <h1 class="title">Retro Rack</h1>
        <div class="quickRow"><button class="btn" id="bRejoin" type="button" hidden></button><button class="btn" id="bQuick" type="button" hidden></button></div>
        <div class="cards">
          <button class="card" type="button" data-go="single" data-art="single"><span class="cardName">Single player</span><span class="cardTxt">Computer or practice</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-go="multi" data-art="multi"><span class="cardName">Multiplayer</span><span class="cardTxt">Online or same device</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-go="locker" data-art="locker"><span class="cardName">Locker</span><span class="cardTxt" id="lockerTxt">Cases and looks</span></button>
        </div>
        <p id="ver"></p>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-single" data-title="Single player" hidden>
        <div class="cards">
          <button class="card" type="button" data-go="game" data-opp="bot" data-art="cpu"><span class="cardName">Vs computer</span><span class="cardTxt">Four skill levels</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-go="practice" data-art="practice"><span class="cardName">Practice</span><span class="cardTxt">Free play, trick shots</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-go="career" data-art="career"><span class="cardName">Career</span><span class="cardTxt">From the pub to the top</span></button>
        </div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-cnew" data-title="New career" hidden>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Name</div><div class="seg"><input id="cName" maxlength="14" placeholder="Your name" autocomplete="nickname"></div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Look</div><div class="seg" id="cStyle"></div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Colours</div><div class="seg cCols"><span class="cColLbl">Skin</span><span class="swatches" id="cSkin"></span><span class="cColLbl">Hair</span><span class="swatches" id="cHair"></span><span class="cColLbl">Shirt</span><span class="swatches" id="cShirt"></span></div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Aim guide</div><div class="seg" id="cGuide"></div><div class="note" id="cGuideTxt"></div></div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-career" data-title="Career" hidden>
        <div class="cTop"><div class="cMe" id="cMe"></div><div class="cTools"><button class="btn" id="cShop" type="button">Locker</button><button class="btn" id="cExport" type="button">Save to file</button><button class="btn" id="cImport" type="button">Load from file</button><button class="btn" id="cRetire" type="button">Retire</button><input type="file" id="cFile" accept=".json,application/json" hidden></div></div>
        <div class="seg cTiers" id="cTiers"></div>
        <div class="cEvents" id="cEvents"></div>
        <div class="note" id="cNote" role="status"></div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-locker" data-title="Locker" hidden>
        <div class="lockTop"><p class="cInfo" id="lockMoney"></p><div class="seg" id="lockTabs"></div></div>
        <div id="lockBody"></div>
        <div class="lockFoot" id="lockPages"></div>
        <div class="note" id="lockNote" role="status"></div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-cevent" hidden>
        <p class="cInfo" id="cInfo"></p>
        <div class="cEv"><div class="bracket" id="cBracket"></div><div class="cOpp" id="cOpp"></div></div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-multi" data-title="Multiplayer" hidden>
        <div class="cards">
          <button class="card" type="button" data-go="online" data-opp="online" data-art="online"><span class="cardName">Online</span><span class="cardTxt">Rooms and invite links</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-go="game" data-opp="friend" data-art="local"><span class="cardName">Same device</span><span class="cardTxt">Take turns</span></button>
        </div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-game" data-title="Choose a game" hidden>
        <div class="cards">
          <button class="card" type="button" data-mode="8ball" data-art="8ball"><span class="cardName">8-ball</span><span class="cardTxt"></span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-mode="9ball" data-art="9ball"><span class="cardName">9-ball</span><span class="cardTxt"></span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-mode="uk8" data-art="uk8"><span class="cardName">Reds &amp; yellows</span><span class="cardTxt"></span></button>
        </div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-setup" hidden>
        <div class="row" id="rowDiff"><div class="lbl">Skill</div><div class="seg" id="mDiff"></div><div class="note" id="diffTxt"></div></div>
        <div class="row" id="rowRoom"><div class="lbl">Room</div><div class="seg" id="mListed"></div><div class="note" id="listedTxt"></div></div>
        <div class="row" id="rowBlack"><div class="lbl">On black</div><div class="seg" id="mBlack" title="Visits after a foul for a player on the black"></div></div>
        <div class="row" id="rowMatch"><div class="lbl">Match</div><div class="seg" id="mRace"></div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Aim guide</div><div class="seg" id="mGuide"></div><div class="note" id="guideTxt"></div></div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-practice" data-title="Practice" hidden>
        <div class="cards">
          <button class="card" type="button" data-rack="8ball" data-art="r8"><span class="cardName">8-ball rack</span><span class="cardTxt">9 ft table</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-rack="9ball" data-art="r9"><span class="cardName">9-ball rack</span><span class="cardTxt">Diamond, 1 to 9</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-rack="uk" data-art="ruk"><span class="cardName">Reds &amp; yellows</span><span class="cardTxt">7 ft table</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-rack="scatter" data-art="scatter"><span class="cardName">Scatter</span><span class="cardTxt">Balls spread out</span></button>
          <button class="card" type="button" data-rack="trick" data-art="trick"><span class="cardName">Trick shots</span><span class="cardTxt">With demos</span></button>
        </div>
      </section>
      <section class="mScreen" id="sc-online" data-title="Online" hidden>
        <div class="onl">
          <label class="field">Your name <input id="netName" maxlength="14" placeholder="Player" autocomplete="nickname"></label>
          <div class="roomsHead"><span>Open rooms</span><span id="roomsLive"></span></div>
          <div id="roomList"></div>
          <div class="seg"><input id="netCode" maxlength="8" placeholder="Room code" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" aria-label="Room code"><button class="btn" id="bJoin" type="button">Join with code</button></div>
          <div class="note warn" id="netNote" role="status"></div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
    <div class="mFoot"><button class="btn" id="bStart" type="button" hidden>Rack 'em up</button><button class="btn" id="bCreate" type="button" hidden>Create a room</button><button class="btn" id="bCareer" type="button" hidden></button><button class="btn" id="bWithdraw" type="button" hidden>Withdraw</button></div>
  </div>
  <div id="menuIcons"><button class="btn iconBtn" id="bMenuSettings" type="button" title="Settings"><span class="vh">Settings</span></button><button class="btn iconBtn" id="bMenuFull" type="button" aria-pressed="false" title="Fullscreen" hidden><span class="vh">Fullscreen</span></button></div>
</div>

<div id="pause" class="overlay" hidden>
  <div id="pausePanel" class="panel" role="dialog" aria-label="Settings">
    <h2>Settings</h2>
    <div class="sGrid">
      <section class="sSec"><h3>Game</h3>
        <div class="row" id="rowSGuide"><div class="lbl">Aim guide</div><div class="seg" id="sGuide"></div><div class="note" id="sGuideTxt"></div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Markers</div><div class="seg"><button class="btn" id="sMarkers"></button></div><div class="note">A pulsing gold halo round the balls you're on, once you have a colour. In 9-ball, the lowest ball.</div></div>
      </section>
      <section class="sSec"><h3>Looks</h3>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Cloth</div><div class="seg picker" id="sCloth"></div><div class="note" id="sClothTxt"></div></div>
        <div class="row" id="rowCue"><div class="lbl">Cue</div><div class="seg picker" id="sCue"></div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Gloves</div><div class="seg picker" id="sGlove"></div></div>
        <div class="row" id="rowDev" hidden><div class="lbl">Dev mode</div><div class="seg"><button class="btn" id="sDev" type="button">Switch off</button></div><div class="note">A test locker with every look. Your own locker comes back when it's off.</div></div>
      </section>
      <section class="sSec"><h3>Display</h3>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Pixel size</div><div class="seg" id="sPixel"></div><div class="note">1× is sharpest; bigger is more retro and easier on phones.</div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Colours</div><div class="seg" id="sLevels"></div><div class="note">Shades per colour channel. Lower is more retro.</div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Dither</div><div class="seg"><button class="btn" id="sDither"></button></div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Outlines</div><div class="seg"><button class="btn" id="sOutline"></button></div></div>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Scanlines</div><div class="seg"><button class="btn" id="sScan"></button></div></div>
      </section>
      <section class="sSec"><h3>Sound and touch</h3>
        <div class="row"><div class="lbl">Sound</div><div class="seg" id="sVolume"></div></div>
        <div class="row" id="rowVibrate"><div class="lbl">Vibration</div><div class="seg"><button class="btn" id="sVibrate"></button></div><div class="note">A short buzz when you pot a ball, a longer one on a foul.</div></div>
      </section>
    </div>
    <div class="actions"><button class="btn" id="bResume">Resume</button><span id="pauseGame"><button class="btn" id="bRestart">Re-rack</button> <button class="btn" id="bOfferRerack">Offer re-rack</button> <button class="btn" id="bConcede">Concede frame</button> <button class="btn" id="bQuit">Quit to menu</button></span></div>
  </div>
</div>

<div id="reel" class="overlay" hidden>
  <div class="panel reelPanel" role="dialog" aria-labelledby="reelTitle">
    <h2 id="reelTitle"></h2>
    <div id="reelWin"><div id="reelStrip"></div><div class="reelMark"></div></div>
    <p id="reelTxt" role="status"></p>
    <div class="actions" id="reelBtns" hidden><button class="btn" id="bReelUse" type="button">Use it</button><button class="btn" id="bReelAgain" type="button">Open another</button><button class="btn" id="bReelDone" type="button">Done</button></div>
  </div>
</div>

<div id="rotate" class="panel" role="status" hidden><p>Turn your phone sideways for a bigger table.</p><button class="btn" id="bRotateOk">OK</button></div>

<div id="ballPick" class="panel" hidden>
  <p>Tap a ball to add it or take it off. Then drag it into place.</p>
  <div id="ballGrid"></div>

</div>

  <div class="panel offerPanel" role="dialog" aria-labelledby="offerTxt">
    <p id="offerTxt"></p>
    <div class="actions"><button class="btn" id="bOfferYes">Accept</button><button class="btn" id="bOfferNo">Decline</button></div>
  </div>
</div>

<div id="lobby" class="overlay" hidden>
  <div id="lobbyPanel" class="panel" role="dialog" aria-labelledby="lobbyTitle">
    <h2 id="lobbyTitle">Online game</h2>
    <div class="lbl">Room code</div>
    <div id="lobbyCode">-----</div>
    <p id="lobbyStatus" role="status">Connecting…</p>
    <input id="lobbyLink" readonly aria-label="Invite link">
    <div class="actions"><button class="btn" id="bCopyLink">Copy invite link</button><button class="btn" id="bLobbyCancel">Cancel</button></div>
    <p class="note" id="lobbyNote"></p>
  </div>
</div>

<div id="over" class="overlay" hidden>
  <div id="overPanel" class="panel" role="dialog" aria-labelledby="overTitle">

    <p id="overWhy"></p>
    <p id="overStats"></p>

  </div>
</div>

<script type="module">__BUNDLE__</script>

</html>
```

## relay/src/index.js

The Cloudflare Worker relay: a Room Durable Object per room code, and one Lobby for the open-rooms list. (180 lines)

```js
// Retro Rack relay: a tiny Cloudflare Worker that passes messages between the two players in a room,
// and keeps a live list of open rooms. It never runs the game itself; each browser checks every shot.
import { DurableObject } from "cloudflare:workers";

const MODES = ["8ball", "9ball", "uk8"];
const cleanName = s => String(s || "").replace(/[\u0000-\u001f<>]/g, "").trim().slice(0, 14) || "Player";
const lobbyStub = env => env.LOBBY.get(env.LOBBY.idFromName("lobby"));

export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const isWs = request.headers.get("Upgrade") === "websocket";
    if (url.pathname === "/lobby") {
      if (!isWs) return new Response("This address expects a WebSocket connection.", { status: 426 });
      return lobbyStub(env).fetch(request);
    }
    const m = url.pathname.match(/^\/room\/([A-Za-z0-9]{3,12})$/);
    if (!m) {
      return new Response("Retro Rack relay is running.", { headers: { "content-type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8" } });
    }
    if (!isWs) return new Response("This address expects a WebSocket connection.", { status: 426 });
    return env.ROOMS.get(env.ROOMS.idFromName(m[1].toUpperCase())).fetch(request);
  },
};

// One Durable Object per room. Uses the WebSocket Hibernation API, so an idle room costs nothing.
export class Room extends DurableObject {
  constructor(ctx, env) {
    super(ctx, env);
    this.ctx.setWebSocketAutoResponse(new WebSocketRequestResponsePair("ping", "pong"));
  }

  live(except) {
    return this.ctx.getWebSockets()
      .filter(ws => ws !== except)
      .map(ws => ({ ws, a: ws.deserializeAttachment() || {} }))
      .filter(s => s.a.seat === 0 || s.a.seat === 1);
  }

  async lobby(op, arg) {
    try { await lobbyStub(this.env)[op](arg); } catch (e) { /* the room still works if the list is unavailable */ }
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const code = (url.pathname.split("/").pop() || "").toUpperCase();
    const cid = (url.searchParams.get("cid") || "").replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/g, "").slice(0, 32) || crypto.randomUUID();
    const { 0: client, 1: server } = new WebSocketPair();
    this.ctx.acceptWebSocket(server);

    let others = this.live(server);
    // the same browser tab reconnecting replaces its old connection and keeps its seat
    let seat = -1;
    for (const s of others) if (s.a.cid === cid) { seat = s.a.seat; s.ws.serializeAttachment({ cid, seat: -1 }); try { s.ws.close(4000, "replaced"); } catch (e) {} }
    others = others.filter(s => s.a.cid !== cid);
    const taken = new Set(others.map(s => s.a.seat));
    const seats = (await this.ctx.storage.get("seats")) || {};
    if (seat < 0) for (const k of [0, 1]) if (seats[k] === cid && !taken.has(k)) seat = k;
    if (seat < 0) for (const k of [0, 1]) if (!taken.has(k)) { seat = k; break; }

    if (seat < 0) {
      server.serializeAttachment({ cid, seat: -1 });
      server.send(JSON.stringify({ t: "full" }));
      server.close(4001, "room full");
      return new Response(null, { status: 101, webSocket: client });
    }
    server.serializeAttachment({ cid, seat });
    seats[seat] = cid;
    await this.ctx.storage.put("seats", seats);
    await this.ctx.storage.deleteAlarm();

    // open-rooms list: a listed room shows while its creator waits, and disappears once a second player joins
    const meta = (await this.ctx.storage.get("meta")) || { code };
    if (others.length > 0) {
      if (!meta.played) { meta.played = true; await this.ctx.storage.put("meta", meta); if (meta.listed) await this.lobby("removeRoom", code); }
    } else if (url.searchParams.get("list") === "1" && !meta.played) {
      const mode = MODES.includes(url.searchParams.get("mode")) ? url.searchParams.get("mode") : "8ball";
      Object.assign(meta, { listed: true, name: cleanName(url.searchParams.get("name")), mode });
      await this.ctx.storage.put("meta", meta);
      await this.lobby("addRoom", { code, name: meta.name, mode });
    }

    server.send(JSON.stringify({ t: "welcome", seat, peer: others.length > 0 }));
    for (const s of others) { try { s.ws.send(JSON.stringify({ t: "peer", on: true })); } catch (e) {} }
    return new Response(null, { status: 101, webSocket: client });
  }

  async webSocketMessage(ws, message) {
    if (typeof message !== "string" || message.length > 20000) return;
    const a = ws.deserializeAttachment() || {};
    if (a.seat !== 0 && a.seat !== 1) return;
    let o;
    try { o = JSON.parse(message); } catch (e) { return; }
    if (!o || typeof o.t !== "string") return;
    o.from = a.seat;
    const out = JSON.stringify(o);
    for (const s of this.live(ws)) if (s.a.seat !== a.seat) { try { s.ws.send(out); } catch (e) {} }
  }

  async webSocketClose(ws, code) {
    await this.gone(ws);
    try { ws.close(code === 1005 || code === 1006 ? 1000 : code, "closing"); } catch (e) {}
  }

  async webSocketError(ws) { await this.gone(ws); }

  async gone(ws) {
    const a = ws.deserializeAttachment() || {};
    const rest = this.live(ws);
    if ((a.seat === 0 || a.seat === 1) && !rest.some(s => s.a.seat === a.seat)) {
      for (const s of rest) { try { s.ws.send(JSON.stringify({ t: "peer", on: false })); } catch (e) {} }
    }
    if (!rest.length) {
      const meta = (await this.ctx.storage.get("meta")) || {};
      if (meta.listed && !meta.played) await this.lobby("removeRoom", meta.code);
      await this.ctx.storage.setAlarm(Date.now() + 6 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    }
  }

  // tidy up rooms nobody has used for six hours
  async alarm() {
    if (!this.ctx.getWebSockets().length) await this.ctx.storage.deleteAll();
  }
}

// A single Durable Object holding the list of open rooms. Menus watch it over a WebSocket and get
// pushed a fresh list whenever a room opens or closes.
export class Lobby extends DurableObject {
  constructor(ctx, env) {
    super(ctx, env);
    this.ctx.setWebSocketAutoResponse(new WebSocketRequestResponsePair("ping", "pong"));
  }

  async rooms() {
    const r = (await this.ctx.storage.get("rooms")) || {};
    const cutoff = Date.now() - 45 * 60 * 1000;          // forget rooms left waiting for 45 minutes
    for (const k of Object.keys(r)) if (r[k].at < cutoff) delete r[k];
    return r;
  }

  view(r) { return Object.values(r).sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at).slice(0, 30); }

  push(r, only) {
    const msg = JSON.stringify({ t: "rooms", rooms: this.view(r), now: Date.now() });
    for (const ws of only ? [only] : this.ctx.getWebSockets()) { try { ws.send(msg); } catch (e) {} }
  }

  async addRoom(e) {
    const r = await this.rooms();
    r[e.code] = { code: e.code, name: cleanName(e.name), mode: MODES.includes(e.mode) ? e.mode : "8ball", at: Date.now() };
    const keys = Object.keys(r).sort((a, b) => r[b].at - r[a].at);
    for (const k of keys.slice(50)) delete r[k];
    await this.ctx.storage.put("rooms", r);
    this.push(r);
  }

  async removeRoom(code) {
    const r = await this.rooms();
    if (!r[code]) return;
    delete r[code];
    await this.ctx.storage.put("rooms", r);
    this.push(r);
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    const { 0: client, 1: server } = new WebSocketPair();
    this.ctx.acceptWebSocket(server);
    this.push(await this.rooms(), server);
    return new Response(null, { status: 101, webSocket: client });
  }

  async webSocketMessage(ws, message) {
    if (message === "list") this.push(await this.rooms(), ws);
  }

  async webSocketClose(ws, code) {
    try { ws.close(code === 1005 || code === 1006 ? 1000 : code, "closing"); } catch (e) {}
  }
}
```

```jsonc
  "name": "retro-rack-relay",
  "main": "src/index.js",
  "compatibility_date": "2025-06-01",

      { "name": "ROOMS", "class_name": "Room" },
      { "name": "LOBBY", "class_name": "Lobby" }
    ]

  // free-plan friendly SQLite-backed Durable Objects (v2 adds the open-rooms list)
  "migrations": [
    { "tag": "v1", "new_sqlite_classes": ["Room"] },
    { "tag": "v2", "new_sqlite_classes": ["Lobby"] }
  ]
}
```

## sw.js

Service worker for the installable app (network first). (44 lines)

```js
// Retro Rack service worker: lets the installed game start without internet.
// Network first, always: when online, every file comes fresh from the site (so updates arrive straight away and
// both online players get the same version); the saved copy is used only when the network fails.
const CACHE = 'retro-rack';
const FILES = ['./', 'config.js', 'manifest.webmanifest', 'icons/icon-192.png', 'icons/icon-512.png'];

self.addEventListener('install', e => {
  e.waitUntil(caches.open(CACHE)
    .then(c => c.addAll(FILES.map(f => new Request(f, { cache: 'no-cache' }))))
    .then(() => self.skipWaiting()));
});

self.addEventListener('activate', e => {
  e.waitUntil(caches.keys()
    .then(keys => Promise.all(keys.filter(k => k !== CACHE).map(k => caches.delete(k))))
    .then(() => self.clients.claim()));
});

self.addEventListener('fetch', e => {
  const req = e.request, url = new URL(req.url);
  if (req.method !== 'GET' || url.origin !== location.origin) return;
  // the game is one page, so every visit to it shares one saved copy, whatever the ?relay= or #room= part says
  const home = new URL('./', self.registration.scope);
  const page = req.mode === 'navigate' && (url.pathname === home.pathname || url.pathname === home.pathname + 'index.html');
  if (req.mode === 'navigate' && !page) return;   // other pages on the site (README and so on) aren't the game
  const key = page ? home.href : req;
  e.respondWith((async () => {
    try {
      // 'no-cache' checks with the site each time (cheap when nothing changed) instead of trusting the browser cache
      let res = await fetch(page ? req.url : req, { cache: 'no-cache', credentials: 'same-origin' });
      if (page && res.redirected) res = await fetch(req);
      if (res.ok && res.type === 'basic') {
        const copy = res.clone();
        e.waitUntil(caches.open(CACHE).then(c => c.put(key, copy)));
      }
      return res;
    } catch (err) {
      const saved = await caches.match(key, { ignoreSearch: true });
      if (saved) return saved;
      throw err;
    }
  })());
});
```
